# Sentence cues: every place read, old and new Korean

The pairs behind the readings in [the evidence](README.md#reading-old-and-new-korean-done-item-15).
Each row is one new sentence: the old cues it overlaps (Old, cues joined by " / ")
and its new parts (New, parts joined by " ‖ "), with the verdict and reason given in the reading.
